# Version-aware consolidated catalog

Snapshot 2026-11-09 · Sole02 owner · inventory and evidence routing, **not final scholarly synthesis**.

[Coverage](QUESTION_COVERAGE.md) · [Counts](COUNTS.md) · [Queue](READING_QUEUE.md) · [Problem records](PROBLEM_MATRIX.md)

## Current override — source audit and actual reading snapshot (2026-11-09)

The earlier discovery tables below are preserved as **historical** provenance, not current reading or venue states. Use [READING_STATUS](READING_STATUS.md) for hash-pinned actual V01–V08 cards; V01/V05 remain in progress. Use the official audit override in section7 below for A07 upgrades and Compiled Tokenizers' withdrawn status. Official API-observed metadata (possibly cached), rendered live thread, source body acquisition, source reading and accepted decision are **five different facts**. No live thread/withdrawal-event timestamp or ICLR2027 acceptance is verified.

The inherited discovery union was772 conservative work/source clusters. The audited separate method-name collision Rvz6kIZAcg adds one candidate: **773 clusters**, not773 verified papers. Same-title public arXiv sources added later are provisional source-edition relations, not extra works or certified identical conference manuscripts. New source routes and next-round IDs are in [SOURCE_ROUTES](SOURCE_ROUTES.md) and [NEXT_ROUND_GAP_MATRIX](NEXT_ROUND_GAP_MATRIX.md).

## Identity and evidence contract

All543 W and141 BIW aliases are retained;52 explicit overlaps yield632 inherited entities. Stable versions/title changes are not new works. The complete inherited index is navigation, not a claim this owner read every original or certified its proofs. Non-academic sources/standards remain sources, not silently academic papers.

EXISTING_FULL_SCOPED means an inherited carrying-card scope, adequate only for bounded uses in QUESTION_COVERAGE. PARTIAL/ABSTRACT_METADATA are not FULL. NEW_VERSION_MISSING retains the old FULL while leaving the newer scope open. Catalog integration adds zero original FULL reads. Current card corrections override stale old-audit unread labels, not source-specific theorem restrictions.

Source verification is inherited from the exact A01–A08 discovery logs; Sole02 made no fresh network verification. A08 official API-search metadata (often cached) is not live direct-forum/body access. A07 indexed records are weaker. No ICLR2027 acceptance is established. Public exact-title clusters conservatively avoid double counting while keeping forum/arXiv versions separate and their equivalence unverified; no author matching/deanonymization.

Stable source IDs and explicit W/BIW aliases, not titles alone, control these merges. BR handles are local v2 locators, not edits to old registries. Every listed scope remains incomplete; some sources have stronger old scoped cards, which remain in section1. Every precise problem/solution/technique/learning-source/visibility/controls/failure/cost record is retained in the English PROBLEM_MATRIX entry.

Two AsymKV works remain distinct; RunningTensor/Triadic, HOLA/LoLA, EDA/GDN-2/Dual DeltaNet and RAM-Net/RAM migration remain distinct. Hydra A04-R02=N06-05; Lucas A01-R16=LD-N08; variance equalization A01-R19=A05-N04; lagging inference=BIW-0096. W0086 title/version changes do not add work entities.

Scratchpad v2 remains a missing-version scope, not a new paper. Turnip/ PriorBPB workshop locators do not certify conference state. W0050 missing supporting_evidence.pdf and W0160 unread report portions remain PARTIAL. Old claim restrictions stay binding; local proof issues do not invalidate unrelated empirical evidence.
